Post-mortem: the backtest/direct red was caused by caller divergence, not detector logic. Backtest built its own history cache and precomputed liquidity map, while the direct path read liquidity and state through separate ad hoc assembly. Canonical `process_bar` now owns bar evaluation, and callers must supply the same bar payload, history, and liquidity snapshot source if they want parity.
